1: import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
2: import type { Metadata } from "next";
3: import {
4:   fetchUniversityBySlug,
5:   fetchAllOnlineUniversitySlugs,
6: } from "@/online-learning/data/api";
7: import UniversityProfilePage from "@/online-learning/components/university-profile/UniversityProfilePage";
8: 
9: interface PageProps {
10:   params: { slug: string };
11: }
12: 
13: export async function generateStaticParams() {
14:   const universities = await fetchAllOnlineUniversitySlugs();
15:   return universities.map((u) => ({ slug: u.slug }));
16: }
17: 
18: export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
19:   const university = await fetchUniversityBySlug(params.slug);
20:   if (!university) {
21:     return { title: "University Not Found | Career Buddy Club" };
22:   }
23: 
24:   const feeText = university.feeRange
25:     ? `₹${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN").format(university.feeRange.min)} – ₹${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN").format(university.feeRange.max)}`
26:     : "";
27: 
28:   const description = `Explore ${university.totalPrograms} online programs at ${university.name}. ${feeText ? `Fees from ${feeText}.` : ""} Compare programs, check eligibility, and get free counselling.`;
29: 
30:   return {
31:     title: `${university.name} Online Programs | Career Buddy Club`,
32:     description,
33:     openGraph: {
34:       title: `${university.name} Online Programs`,
35:       description,
36:       type: "website",
37:       siteName: "Career Buddy Club",
38:     },
39:   };
40: }
41: 
42: export default async function OnlineUniversityPage({ params }: PageProps) {
43:   const university = await fetchUniversityBySlug(params.slug);
44: 
45:   if (!university) {
46:     notFound();
47:   }
48:

50: }

1: "use client";
2: 
3: import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
4: import { X, Check } from 'lucide-react';
5: 
6: interface Program {
7:   id: number | string;
8:   name: string;
9: }
10: 
11: interface EnquiryFormProps {
12:   isOpen: boolean;
13:   onClose: () => void;
14:   universityName: string;
15:   programs: Program[];
16:   preselectedProgram?: string;
17: }
18: 
19: export default function EnquiryForm({ isOpen, onClose, universityName, programs, preselectedProgram }: EnquiryFormProps) {
20:   const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'submitting' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle');
21:   const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
22:     name: '',
23:     phone: '',
24:     email: '',
25:     program: preselectedProgram || '',
26:     city: '',
27:     consent: false
28:   });
29: 
30:   useEffect(() => {
31:     if (preselectedProgram) {
32:       setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, program: preselectedProgram }));
33:     }
34:   }, [preselectedProgram]);
35: 
36:   if (!isOpen) return null;
37: 
38:   const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
39:     const { name, value, type } = e.target;
40:     setFormData(prev => ({
41:       ...prev,
42:       [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked : value
43:     }));
44:   };
45: 
46:   const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
47:     e.preventDefault();
48:     setStatus('submitting');
49:     
50:     // Simulate API call
51:     setTimeout(() => {
52:       setStatus('success');
53:       setTimeout(() => {
54:         setStatus('idle');
55:         setFormData({
56:           name: '', phone: '', email: '', program: preselectedProgram || '', city: '', consent: false
57:         });
58:         onClose();
59:       }, 3000);
60:     }, 2000);
61:   };
62: 
63:   const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
64:     if (e.target === e.currentTarget) {
65:       onClose();
66:     }
67:   };
68: 
69:   return (
70:     <div className="upEnquiryOverlay" onClick={handleOverlayClick}>
71:       <div className="upEnquiryModal">
72:         <button className="upEnquiryClose" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
73:           <X size={24} />
74:         </button>
75:         <h2 className="upEnquiryTitle">Enquire Now</h2>
76:         <p>Get details about {universityName}</p>
77: 
78:         {status === 'success' ? (
79:           <div className="upFormSuccess">
80:             <Check size={48} color="green" style={{ margin: '0 auto 16px' }} />
81:             <p>Thank you! Our counsellor will contact you within 24 hours.</p>
82:           </div>
83:         ) : (
84:           <form className="upEnquiryForm" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
85:             <div className="upFormGroup">
86:               <label htmlFor="name" className="upFormLabel">Full Name *</label>
87:               <input type="text" id="name" name="name" className="upFormInput" required value={formData.name} onChange={handleChange} />
88:             </div>
89: 
90:             <div className="upFormGroup">
91:               <label htmlFor="phone" className="upFormLabel">Mobile Number *</label>
92:               <input type="tel" id="phone" name="phone" className="upFormInput" required pattern="[0-9]{10}" title="10 digit mobile number" value={formData.phone} onChange={handleChange} />
93:             </div>
94: 
95:             <div className="upFormGroup">
96:               <label htmlFor="email" className="upFormLabel">Email *</label>
97:               <input type="email" id="email" name="email" className="upFormInput" required value={formData.email} onChange={handleChange} />
98:             </div>
99: 
100:             <div className="upFormGroup">
101:               <label htmlFor="program" className="upFormLabel">Program of Interest</label>
102:               <select id="program" name="program" className="upFormSelect" required value={formData.program} onChange={handleChange}>
103:                 <option value="">Select a Program</option>
104:                 {programs.map(p => (
105:                   <option key={p.id} value={p.name}>{p.name}</option>
106:                 ))}
107:               </select>
108:             </div>
109: 
110:             <div className="upFormGroup">
111:               <label htmlFor="city" className="upFormLabel">City *</label>
112:               <input type="text" id="city" name="city" className="upFormInput" required value={formData.city} onChange={handleChange} />
113:             </div>
114: 
115:             <div className="upFormGroup">
116:               <label className="upFormCheckboxLabel">
117:                 <input type="checkbox" name="consent" className="upFormCheckbox" required checked={formData.consent} onChange={handleChange} />
118:                 I agree to receive counselling calls, emails, and updates. View Privacy Policy.
119:               </label>
120:             </div>
121: 
122:             {status === 'error' && (
123:               <div className="upFormError">Something went wrong. Please try again.</div>

1: import React from "react";
2: import { UniversityCourse } from "@/online-learning/data/api";
3: 
4: interface FeesSectionProps {
5:   courses: UniversityCourse[];
6:   universityName: string;
7: }
8: 
9: export const FeesSection: React.FC<FeesSectionProps> = ({ courses, universityName }) => {
10:   const formatFee = (fee: string) => {
11:     let parsedFee = parseFloat(fee.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ""));
12:     if (isNaN(parsedFee)) return fee;
13: 
14:     const lowerFee = fee.toLowerCase();
15:     if (lowerFee.includes("l")) {
16:       parsedFee = parsedFee * 100000;
17:     } else if (lowerFee.includes("k")) {
18:       parsedFee = parsedFee * 1000;
19:     } else {
20:       if (parsedFee < 10) {
21:         parsedFee = parsedFee * 100000;
22:       } else if (parsedFee >= 10 && parsedFee < 1000) {
23:         parsedFee = parsedFee * 1000;
24:       }
25:     }
26: 
27:     return "₹" + new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN").format(parsedFee);
28:   };
29: 
30:   const coursesWithFees = courses.filter((course) => course.fee && course.fee.trim() !== "");
31: 
32:   return (
33:     <section id="fees" className="upFees">
34:       <h2>Fees & Financial Information</h2>
35: 
36:       {coursesWithFees.length === 0 ? (
37:         <div className="upFeeEmpty">
38:           Fee details are currently not available. Contact us for the latest fee information.
39:         </div>
40:       ) : (
41:         <table className="upFeeTable">
42:           <thead className="upFeeTableHead">
43:             <tr>
44:               <th>Program Name</th>
45:               <th>Duration</th>
46:               <th>Fees</th>
47:             </tr>
48:           </thead>
49:           <tbody>
50:             {coursesWithFees.map((course) => (
51:               <tr key={course.id} className="upFeeTableRow">

29:   { id: "overview", label: "Overview" },
30:   { id: "programs", label: "Programs" },
31:   { id: "fees", label: "Fees" },
32:   { id: "eligibility", label: "Eligibility" },
33:   { id: "accreditations", label: "Accreditations" },

37: ];

39: export default function UniversityProfilePage({ university }: Props) {

41:   const [preselectedProgram, setPreselectedProgram] = useState<string>("");
42:   const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState("overview");
43: 
44:   const openEnquiry = useCallback((programName?: string) => {
45:     if (programName) setPreselectedProgram(programName);
46:     else setPreselectedProgram("");
47:     setEnquiryOpen(true);

49:

51:   useEffect(() => {
52:     const handleScroll = () => {
53:       const offset = 120;
54:       for (let i = SECTIONS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
55:         const el = document.getElementById(SECTIONS[i].id);
56:         if (el) {
57:           const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
58:           if (rect.top <= offset) {
59:             setActiveSection(SECTIONS[i].id);
60:             return;
61:           }
62:         }
63:       }
64:       setActiveSection(SECTIONS[0].id);
65:     };
66: 
67:     window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
68:     return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);
69:   }, []);
70:

72:

79:         onEnquiryOpen={() => openEnquiry()}

84:       <OverviewSection universityName={university.name} />
85: 
86:       <HighlightsGrid universityName={university.name} />
87: 
88: 
89:       <ProgramCatalog
90:         courses={university.courses}
91:         onRequestDetails={(name: string) => openEnquiry(name)}
92:       />
93: 
94:       {featuredCourse && (
95:         <FeaturedProgram
96:           course={featuredCourse}
97:           universityName={university.name}
98:         />
99:       )}
100: 
101:       <ProgramComparison courses={university.courses} />

1: // ============================================================================
2: //  CareerWise — scoped stylesheet
3: // ============================================================================
4: //  careerbuddyclub loads Bootstrap 5 plus public/assets/scss/style.scss
5: //  globally from the root layout. Those sheets restyle bare elements
6: //  (body 20px / EB Garamond, h1 85px, h2 70px, p 16px/1.8, a underlined) and
7: //  define .navbar and .card, all of which would deform the CareerWise design.
8: //
9: //  Everything CareerWise renders is wrapped in `.cw-root`, and this file
10: //  nests the whole CareerWise stylesheet inside that class. Two things follow:
11: //
12: //    1. Every CareerWise selector gains one class of specificity, so
13: //       `.cw-root .navbar` (0,2,0) beats Bootstrap's `.navbar` (0,1,0) and
14: //       `.cw-root .hero h1` (0,2,1) beats the reset below.
15: //    2. The reset below re-establishes, inside the scope only, exactly the
16: //       baseline CareerWise's own globals.css provided — nothing more. Values
17: //       are deliberately browser defaults, not new opinions, so the port looks
18: //       identical to the standalone app.
19: //
20: //  Nothing here escapes `.cw-root`, so no existing careerbuddyclub page is
21: //  affected.
22: // ============================================================================
23: 
24: @use "sass:meta";
25: 
26: .cw-root {
27:   // --- typography baseline (was: CareerWise `body`) ---------------------
28:   --font-geist-sans: "Geist", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI",
29:     Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
30:   --font-geist-mono: "Geist Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo,
31:     Consolas, monospace;
32:   --background: #ffffff;
33:   --foreground: #122036;
34: 
35:   box-sizing: border-box;
36:   // NOTE: CareerWise set `max-width: 100vw; overflow-x: hidden` on html/body,
37:   // where overflow propagates to the viewport. On a plain <div> the same
38:   // declarations would instead create a scroll container and break
39:   // `position: sticky` descendants, so they are deliberately omitted here —
40:   // CareerWise's own `.page { overflow: hidden }` already clips the bleed.
41:   color: var(--foreground);
42:   background: var(--background);
43:   font-family: var(--font-geist-sans);
44:   font-size: 16px;
45:   font-weight: 400;
46:   font-style: normal;
47:   line-height: 1.5;
48:   letter-spacing: normal;
49:   text-align: left;
50:   text-transform: none;
51:   -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
52:   -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
53: 
54:   // --- CareerWise globals.css `* { margin:0; padding:0; box-sizing }` ----
55:   *,
56:   *::before,
57:   *::after {
58:     box-sizing: border-box;
59:   }
60: 
61:   * {
62:     margin: 0;
63:     padding: 0;
64:   }
65: 
66:   // --- undo careerbuddyclub's bare-element rules, inside the scope only --
67:   // Values are the user-agent defaults CareerWise was designed against.
68:   h1,
69:   h2,
70:   h3,
71:   h4,
72:   h5,
73:   h6 {
74:     font-family: inherit;
75:     font-weight: 700;
76:     color: inherit;
77:     line-height: 1.2;
78:     letter-spacing: normal;
79:   }
80: 
81:   h1 { font-size: 2em; }

84:   h4 { font-size: 1em; }
85:   h5 { font-size: 0.83em; }
86:   h6 { font-size: 0.67em; }
87: 
88:   p {
89:     font-family: inherit;
90:     font-size: inherit;
91:     line-height: inherit;
92:     color: inherit;
93:   }
94: 
95:   // CareerWise globals.css: `a { color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }`
96:   a {
97:     color: inherit;
98:     text-decoration: none;
99:   }
100: 
101:   ul,
102:   ol {
103:     list-style-position: outside;

1: /**
2:  * Chrome + style scope for every CareerWise route.
3:  *
4:  * `.cw-root` is the isolation boundary described in
5:  * src/careerwise/styles/online-learning.scss — it must wrap all CareerWise markup,
6:  * including the header and footer, or the globally loaded Bootstrap /
7:  * careerbuddyclub stylesheets will restyle it.
8:  *
9:  * `.page` is CareerWise's own outer container (background gradient, min
10:  * height), kept exactly as it was in the standalone app's root layout.
11:  *
12:  * This is a server component: pages that use it can still export `metadata`.
13:  */
14: 
15: import React from "react";
16: import "./styles/online-learning.scss";
17: import { AuthProvider } from "@/online-learning/context/AuthContext";
18: import Header from "@/online-learning/layout/header/Header";
19: import Footer from "@/online-learning/layout/footer/Footer";
20: import { fetchOnlineStreams } from "./data/api";
21: 
22: interface OnlineLearningShellProps {
23:   children: React.ReactNode;
24:   /** Full-bleed routes (e.g. the advisor flow) render without header/footer. */
25:   chrome?: boolean;
26:   showHeader?: boolean;
27:   showFooter?: boolean;
28: }
29: 
30: export default async function OnlineLearningShell({
31:   children,
32:   chrome = true,
33:   showHeader = true,
34:   showFooter = true,
35: }: OnlineLearningShellProps) {
36:   const isHeaderVisible = chrome && showHeader;
37:   const isFooterVisible = chrome && showFooter;
38:   
39:   const streams = isHeaderVisible ? await fetchOnlineStreams() : [];
40: 
41:   return (
42:     <div className="cw-root">
43:       <AuthProvider>
44:         <div className="page">
45:           {isHeaderVisible && <Header initialStreams={streams} />}
46:           {children}
47:           {isFooterVisible && <Footer />}
48:         </div>
49:       </AuthProvider>
50:     </div>
51:   );
52: }

26: export async function fetchOnlineStreams(): Promise<Stream[]> {

28:     const res = await fetch("https://test.careerbuddyclub.com:8080/api/students/getfilterationdata", {
29:       method: "POST",
30:       headers: {
31:         "Content-Type": "application/json",
32:       },
33:       next: { revalidate: 3600 } // cache for 1 hour
34:     });
35: 
36:     if (!res.ok) {
37:       throw new Error(`Failed to fetch filtration data: ${res.statusText}`);
38:     }
39: 
40:     const data: FilterationData = await res.json();

49:     }));

52:   } catch (error) {

99: export function slugify(name: string): string {
100:   return name
101:     .toLowerCase()
102:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
103:     .replace(/^-|-$/g, "");

117:   id: number;
118:   slug: string;
119:   name: string;
120:   courses: UniversityCourse[];
121:   streamCategories: string[];
122:   totalPrograms: number;
123:   feeRange: { min: number; max: number } | null;
124: }
125: 
126: function inferDegreeLevel(name: string): string {
127:   const n = name.toLowerCase();
128:   if (/\bph\.?d\b|\bdoctoral\b/.test(n)) return "PhD";
129:   if (/\bdiploma\b/.test(n)) return "Diploma";
130:   if (/\bcertificate\b/.test(n)) return "Certificate";
131:   if (
132:     /\b(m\.?a|m\.?b\.?a|m\.?s\.?c|m\.?com|m\.?tech|m\.?c\.?a|pgdm|pgpm|pgp|executive|mhrm|mib)\b/.test(n) ||
133:     /\bp\.?g\.?\s/.test(n)
134:   )
135:     return "PG";
136:   if (
137:     /\b(b\.?a|b\.?b\.?a|b\.?s\.?c|b\.?com|b\.?tech|b\.?c\.?a|b\.?m\.?s|b\.?h\.?m|b\.?p\.?t)\b/.test(n)
138:   )
139:     return "UG";
140:   return "Other";
141: }
142: 
143: export async function fetchUniversityBySlug(
144:   slug: string
145: ): Promise<UniversityProfile | null> {
146:   try {
147:     const [streams, allColleges] = await Promise.all([
148:       fetchOnlineStreams(),
149:       fetchAllCollegesDetails(),
150:     ]);
151: 
152:     const onlineCollegeIds = new Set<number>();
153:     streams.forEach((s) => s.colleges?.forEach((c) => onlineCollegeIds.add(c.id)));
154: 
155:     const college = allColleges.find(
156:       (c) => onlineCollegeIds.has(c.id) && slugify(c.college_full_name) === slug
157:     );
158:     if (!college) return null;
159: 
160:     const courseStreamMap = new Map<number, string>();
161:     streams.forEach((s) =>
162:       s.courses?.forEach((c) => courseStreamMap.set(c.id, s.title))
163:     );
164: 
165:     const courses: UniversityCourse[] = (college.courses || []).map((c) => ({
166:       id: c.id,
167:       name: c.name,
168:       duration: c.duration,
169:       fee: c.pivot?.fee,
170:       streamCategory: courseStreamMap.get(c.id),
171:       degreeLevel: inferDegreeLevel(c.name),
172:     }));
173: 
174:     const fees = courses
175:       .map((c) => parseFloat(c.fee || ""))
176:       .filter((f) => !isNaN(f) && f > 0);
177:     const feeRange =
178:       fees.length > 0
179:         ? { min: Math.min(...fees), max: Math.max(...fees) }
180:         : null;
181: 
182:     const streamCategories = [
183:       ...new Set(courses.map((c) => c.streamCategory).filter(Boolean)),
184:     ] as string[];
185: 
186:     return {
187:       id: college.id,
188:       slug,
189:       name: college.college_full_name,
190:       courses,
191:       streamCategories,
192:       totalPrograms: courses.length,
193:       feeRange,
194:     };
195:   } catch (error) {
196:     console.error("Error fetching university profile:", error);
197:     return null;
198:   }
199: }
200: 
201: export async function fetchAllOnlineUniversitySlugs(): Promise<
202:   { slug: string; name: string }[]
203: > {
204:   try {
205:     const streams = await fetchOnlineStreams();
206:     const seen = new Map<number, string>();
207:     streams.forEach((s) =>
208:       s.colleges?.forEach((c) => {
209:         if (!seen.has(c.id)) seen.set(c.id, c.college_full_name);
210:       })
211:     );
212:     return Array.from(seen.values()).map((name) => ({
213:       slug: slugify(name),
214:       name,
215:     }));
216:   } catch (error) {
217:     console.error("Error fetching university slugs:", error);
218:     return [];
219:   }
220: }

1: "use client";

5: 
6: interface Program {
7:   id: number | string;
8:   name: string;
9: }
10: 
11: interface EnquiryFormProps {
12:   isOpen: boolean;
13:   onClose: () => void;
14:   universityName: string;
15:   programs: Program[];
16:   preselectedProgram?: string;
17: }
18: 
19: export default function EnquiryForm({ isOpen, onClose, universityName, programs, preselectedProgram }: EnquiryFormProps) {
20:   const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'submitting' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle');
21:   const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
22:     name: '',
23:     phone: '',
24:     email: '',
25:     program: preselectedProgram || '',
26:     city: '',
27:     consent: false
28:   });
29: 
30:   useEffect(() => {
31:     if (preselectedProgram) {
32:       setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, program: preselectedProgram }));
33:     }
34:   }, [preselectedProgram]);
35: 
36:   if (!isOpen) return null;
37: 
38:   const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
39:     const { name, value, type } = e.target;
40:     setFormData(prev => ({
41:       ...prev,
42:       [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked : value
43:     }));
44:   };
45: 
46:   const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
47:     e.preventDefault();
48:     setStatus('submitting');
49:     
50:     // Simulate API call
51:     setTimeout(() => {
52:       setStatus('success');
53:       setTimeout(() => {
54:         setStatus('idle');
55:         setFormData({
56:           name: '', phone: '', email: '', program: preselectedProgram || '', city: '', consent: false
57:         });
58:         onClose();
59:       }, 3000);
60:     }, 2000);
61:   };
62: 
63:   const handleOverlayClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
64:     if (e.target === e.currentTarget) {
65:       onClose();
66:     }
67:   };
68: 
69:   return (
70:     <div className="upEnquiryOverlay" onClick={handleOverlayClick}>
71:       <div className="upEnquiryModal">

82:           </div>
83:         ) : (

86:               <label htmlFor="name" className="upFormLabel">Full Name *</label>

1: import React from "react";
2: import { UniversityCourse } from "@/online-learning/data/api";
3: 
4: interface FeesSectionProps {
5:   courses: UniversityCourse[];
6:   universityName: string;
7: }
8: 
9: export const FeesSection: React.FC<FeesSectionProps> = ({ courses, universityName }) => {
10:   const formatFee = (fee: string) => {
11:     let parsedFee = parseFloat(fee.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ""));
12:     if (isNaN(parsedFee)) return fee;
13: 
14:     const lowerFee = fee.toLowerCase();
15:     if (lowerFee.includes("l")) {
16:       parsedFee = parsedFee * 100000;
17:     } else if (lowerFee.includes("k")) {
18:       parsedFee = parsedFee * 1000;
19:     } else {
20:       if (parsedFee < 10) {
21:         parsedFee = parsedFee * 100000;
22:       } else if (parsedFee >= 10 && parsedFee < 1000) {
23:         parsedFee = parsedFee * 1000;
24:       }
25:     }
26: 
27:     return "₹" + new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN").format(parsedFee);
28:   };
29: 
30:   const coursesWithFees = courses.filter((course) => course.fee && course.fee.trim() !== "");
31: 
32:   return (
33:     <section id="fees" className="upFees">
34:       <h2>Fees & Financial Information</h2>
35: 
36:       {coursesWithFees.length === 0 ? (
37:         <div className="upFeeEmpty">
38:           Fee details are currently not available. Contact us for the latest fee information.
39:         </div>
40:       ) : (
41:         <table className="upFeeTable">
42:           <thead className="upFeeTableHead">
43:             <tr>
44:               <th>Program Name</th>
45:               <th>Duration</th>
46:               <th>Fees</th>
47:             </tr>
48:           </thead>
49:           <tbody>
50:             {coursesWithFees.map((course) => (
51:               <tr key={course.id} className="upFeeTableRow">
52:                 <td className="upFeeTableCell">{course.name}</td>
53:                 <td className="upFeeTableCell">{course.duration || "N/A"}</td>
54:                 <td className="upFeeTableCell">{formatFee(course.fee as string)}</td>
55:               </tr>
56:             ))}
57:           </tbody>

59:       )}
60:     </section>
61:   );

13:   const toggleSelection = (index: number) => {
14:     setSelectedPrograms((prev) => {
15:       if (prev.includes(index)) {
16:         return prev.filter((i) => i !== index);
17:       }
18:       if (prev.length >= 3) {

20:         return prev;
21:       }
22:       return [...prev, index];

26: }

28: const SECTIONS = [
29:   { id: "overview", label: "Overview" },
30:   { id: "programs", label: "Programs" },
31:   { id: "fees", label: "Fees" },
32:   { id: "eligibility", label: "Eligibility" },
33:   { id: "accreditations", label: "Accreditations" },

41:   const [preselectedProgram, setPreselectedProgram] = useState<string>("");
42:   const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState("overview");

45:     if (programName) setPreselectedProgram(programName);
46:     else setPreselectedProgram("");

48:   }, []);

51:   useEffect(() => {
52:     const handleScroll = () => {
53:       const offset = 120;

55:         const el = document.getElementById(SECTIONS[i].id);
56:         if (el) {
57:           const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
58:           if (rect.top <= offset) {
59:             setActiveSection(SECTIONS[i].id);
60:             return;
61:           }
62:         }
63:       }
64:       setActiveSection(SECTIONS[0].id);
65:     };
66: 
67:     window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
68:     return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);
69:   }, []);
